<guidelines>
Follow these behavioral and workflow guidelines at all times:
# Documentation
- Maintain documentation integrity. Preserve all existing comments and docstrings that are unrelated to your code changes, unless the user specifies otherwise.

# Obey Explicit Directives
If the user specifies precise quantitative filtering rules, layout boundaries, or architectural preferences, enforce them exactly as requested without alteration.

# Never Guess Code Logic, Schemas, or File Paths
NEVER infer implementation details, variable names, or file locations without inspecting the authoritative source using code search and file viewing tools.

# Inspect Logs & Stack Traces Before Diagnosing Errors
NEVER form a diagnostic hypothesis for a runtime failure, or test breakage, without reading the full, un-truncated error log. When an error occurs, your VERY FIRST ACTION must be to fetch and read the exact logs. Base your diagnosis strictly on empirical log evidence.

# No Superficial Symptom Patches
NEVER resolve errors by masking symptoms, swallowing exceptions, returning dummy fallbacks, commenting out broken assertions, or deleting failing unit tests. When a test or function fails, identify why the underlying contract was broken. If an API returns missing or null data, trace the upstream data provider instead of wrapping the call in a silent try/except or returning an empty 0-byte ArrayBuffer.

# Never Declare Success Without Running Verification Commands
NEVER claim a task is resolved, a bug is fixed, or a feature is working until you have gathered concrete, empirical runtime verification demonstrating clean success. Editing a file does not equal completing the task. You MUST run the build or test command afterwards.

# Never Ignore Explicit Command Failures or Error Exit Codes
If a command fails, you MUST explicitly acknowledge the failure to the user or continue debugging. Never gloss over a build timeout or permission denied error by focusing only on the part of the code that compiled.

# Check Feature Flags & Enforce Strict Control Flow Scoping
Whenever modifying conditional branches, adding experimental features, or processing loops, ensure that new logic is strictly scoped and evaluated against all possible execution paths.

# Preserve Existing API Contracts & Avoid Unintended Side Effects
If you modify a function signature, use code search to find and update every invocation site so the parameter is actually passed.

# Silent Log Inspection & Professional Synthesis
When background tasks (run_command async, manage_task, schedule) complete or emit log notifications, inspect the log files silently. Summarize and synthesize the exact findings in clean, professional natural language.

# No Snippet Tunnel Vision
Never infer the definition of data structures (proto, struct, class, or enum schemas) from partial file views (first 15 lines or L40-L65 snippets) or design doc text.
If view_file output indicates truncation or if an imported schema is referenced, you MUST adjust StartLine/EndLine or ContentOffset to inspect the complete, exact definition of the target symbols before writing code that consumes them.

# Check Command Registries
Whenever modifying core C/C++/Java command implementations (CLIENT LIST, CLIENT KILL), explicitly search for and update corresponding command definitions across all registry files (commands.def, JSON schemas, .bzl build manifests).

# Audit Before Re-inventing
Search the codebase and recent commit history for pre-existing utility classes or decoupled architecture before writing custom helper classes from scratch.

# No Blocking Calls on Main Looper Threads
Never invoke blocking thread synchronizations (webLatch.await(500, ...), Future.get()) on main Android UI loops or single-threaded event dispatchers. 

# Thread Pool Shutdown Safety
When modifying worker thread loops or shared queues, ensure emergency stop/shutdown signals and loop termination criteria remain intact so thread join operations never deadlock.

# Exact Argument Structure
Pass arguments exactly as expected by the API (calculateRoute({ origin, destination, travelMode }) vs calculateRoute(origin, destination, travelMode)).

# Local State Mutation Only
Do not mutate private third-party DOM properties. Do not push incomplete draft objects directly into global array states; keep transient state within local component state.

# Traceback Justification Required
Every code or configuration edit during debugging MUST be justified by an explicit error traceback, log line, or verified root cause. If the root cause is unknown, investigate further before mutating code.

# Analyze Before Retrying
Never repeat the exact same broken test or shell command line with duplicate/conflicting arguments without analyzing and resolving why the previous command failed.

# Persevere on Log Extraction
If a log retrieval command fails, NEVER abandon log extraction to diagnose blindly. Immediately switch to alternative tools to inspect the actual failure traceback.

# Verify Signatures & Prop Names
Check exact variable names, component prop keys, and method signatures before passing them. Prevent NullPointerException, AttributeError, KeyError, and ReferenceError crashes by explicitly verifying object initialization and non-null states before property dereferencing (layer._path, stat.owner()).

# Dynamic Layout Math
Avoid hardcoding static pixel offsets (+ 12) or arbitrary multipliers (pill_font_size * 2.0) when computing dynamic UI layout heights; calculate exact container bounds from wrapped elements.

</guidelines>

MVP 目标\n24\t\n25\t1. 支持用户从系统分享入口快速导入 PDF、Markdown 和网页。\n26\t2. 将不同来源的内容统一转换为可手写批注的 PDF 文档。\n27\t3. 提供接近 Apple 系统绘图工具体验的基础手写批注能力。\n28\t4. 自动、可靠地保存批注，并允许用户导出包含批注的 PDF。\n29\t5. 在文件列表中按照最后活动时间快速找到最近处理的文档。\n30\t\n31\t## 3. 非目标\n32\t\n33\t以下能力不属于 MVP：\n34\t\n35\t- Markdown 编辑与原文管理。\n36\t- Markdown 或网页内容变化后的自动同步。\n37\t- PDF 文本高亮、下划线、删除线、文本备注等语义批注。\n38\t- OCR 与扫描 PDF 文字识别。\n39\t- 网页像素级还原。\n40\t- 网页批注与最新网页内容自动对齐。\n41\t- 多人协作、评论与分享链接。\n42\t- 账号系统与跨设备同步。\n43\t- 文件夹、标签等复杂文档组织能力。\n44\t- 导出后仍可在第三方 PDF 工具中编辑的标准 PDF Annotation。\n45\t\n46\t## 4. 核心概念\n47\t\n48\t### 4.1 文档来源类型\n49\t\n50\t- PDF\n51\t- Markdown\n52\t- Web\n53\t\n54\t### 4.2 文档状态\n55\t\n56\t| 状态 | 含义 | 点击行为 |\n57\t|---|---|---|\n58\t| importing | 正在复制导入内容 | 展示进度，不进入批注页 |\n59\t| converting | Markdown 或网页正在生成 PDF | 展示转换状态 |\n60\t| webReady | 网页已保存 URL，尚未生成 PDF | 打开内置浏览器 |\n61\t| annotatable | PDF 已准备完成 | 进入批注模式 |\n62\t| failed | 导入或转换失败 | 展示失败原因和重试入口 |\n63\t| corrupted | PDF 无法读取或文件损坏 | 展示错误，并提供删除或重新导入提示 |\n64\t\n65\t### 4.3 最后活动时间\n66\t\n67\t以下操作更新最后活动时间：\n68\t\n69\t- 文件成功导入。\n70\t- Markdown 或网页成功生成 PDF。\n71\t- 用户新增、修改或删除批注。\n72\t- 用户重命名文档。\n73\t\n74\t以下操作不更新最后活动时间：\n75\t\n76\t- 仅打开文档、翻页、滚动或缩放。\n77\t- 导出或分享文档。\n78\t- 后台自动保存。\n79\t- 后台生成缩略图。\n80\t\n81\t## 5. 用户入口与核心流程\n82\t\n83\t### 5.1 App 主入口\n84\t\n85\t1. 用户打开 NoteAnywhere。\n86\t2. App 展示文件列表。\n87\t3. 文件默认按照最后活动时间倒序排列。\n88\t4. 用户点击可批注文档，进入批注模式。\n89\t5. 用户点击尚未捕获的网页，进入内置浏览器。\n90\t6. 用户点击失败条目，查看失败原因并重试。\n91\t\n92\t### 5.2 分享入口\n93\t\n94\tNoteAnywhere 作为系统分享目标，支持接收：\n95\t\n96\t- `.pdf`\n97\t- `.md`\n98\t- `.markdown`\n99\t- Safari 分享的 HTTP/HTTPS URL\n100\t"

  "CodeContent": "# NoteAnywhere\n\n<p align=\"center\">\n  <b>以 PDF 为统一载体、支持网页/Markdown/PDF 交互与 Apple Pencil 手写批注的 iOS / iPadOS 应用</b>\n</p>\n\n---\n\n## 📌 项目概述 (Overview)\n\n**NoteAnywhere** 是一款专注于阅读与高自由度手写批注的 iOS/iPadOS 应用（优先针对 iPad 与 Apple Pencil 深度优化）。\n\n项目旨在打破网页、文档、PDF 之间的媒介壁垒：\n- **PDF**: 从系统分享或文件导入后直接进入批注模式。\n- **Markdown**: 动态渲染并转化为 PDF 快照后提供手写批注。\n- **Web 网页**: 在内置浏览器中实时交互（展开折叠、切页、点击），并支持** Apple Pencil 在动态 DOM 元素上进行实时手写锚定批注**。\n\n目前项目包含完整的产品需求文档（MVP PRD）与高完成度的技术验证原型 **`NoteAnywhereWebPrototype`**。\n\n---\n\n## ✨ 原型核心特性 (Prototype Features)\n\n当前原型项目 `NoteAnywhereWebPrototype` 成功验证了 **“可交互网页 + Apple Pencil DOM 锚定笔迹”** 的核心难题：\n\n1. **手触与笔触解耦 (Dual-Input Coexistence)**\n   - 自定义 `PencilStrokeGestureRecognizer` 仅捕获 Apple Pencil 触摸事件，完全不拦截手指事件。\n   - 手指可流畅滚动、点击、双指缩放（Pinch-Zoom）网页，Apple Pencil 随时落笔书写。\n   - 接入 Apple Pencil `predictedTouches` API，极大降低渲染与跟手延迟。\n\n2. **DOM 实时锚定与跟随 (DOM Anchoring & Transform Tracking)**\n   - 落笔瞬间触发 `document.elementFromPoint` 发起 DOM 锚定，与笔迹采集并行执行。\n   - 笔迹存储为相对于锚点 DOM 元素的相对 CSS 偏移坐标。\n   - 页面滚动与缩放时通过 `CAShapeLayer` / `CALayer` 的 `transform` 实现帧级跟随，零卡顿重绘。\n\n3. **W3C 标准 Selector 容错匹配链 (W3C Selector & Resilient Recovery)**\n   - 锚点序列化遵循 W3C Web Annotation 标准（`CssSelector` + `TextQuoteSelector`）。\n   - 页面 DOM 结构变动时按降级链恢复：`结构定位` ➔ `局部文本搜索` ➔ `全文文本搜索` ➔ `孤儿批注`（隐藏但完整保留数据）。\n\n4. **局部精准监听与沙盒隔离 (Scoped Observer & JS Isolation)**\n   - `MutationObserver` 与 `ResizeObserver` 仅监听已锚定元素及其祖先链，无笔迹时不占用 CPU/内存资源。\n   - 所有 JavaScript 桥接代码均注入在 `WKContentWorld.defaultClient` 隔离世界，防止网页脚本探测与覆盖。\n\n5. **SPA 导航与状态管理 (SPA Navigation & State Handling)**\n   - 通过 `WKWebView.url` 观察及 pushState/replaceState KVO 识别 SPA 单页应用导航。\n   - 切页时自动收起旧页笔迹并释放 JS 侧锚点，返回时重新恢复。\n\n---\n\n## 🛠 架构设计与源码结构 (Architecture)\n\n```\nNoteAnywhere/\n├── NoteAnywhereWebPrototype/            # iOS App 原型核心源码\n│   ├── NoteAnywhereWebPrototypeApp.swift  # SwiftUI 应用入口\n│   ├── HomeView.swift                   # 首页（批注记录列表与快捷测试入口）\n│   ├── WebAnnotationViewController.swift # 核心视图控制器 (UIKit + WKWebView + Pencil Overlay)\n│   ├── InkOverlayView.swift             # 笔迹绘制覆盖层 (CAShapeLayer / CALayer)\n│   ├── PencilStrokeGestureRecognizer.swift # 独立 Pencil 手势识别器\n│   ├── PencilKitInk.swift / SelfCheck   # PencilKit 墨迹集成与自检\n│   ├── InkTool.swift / InkPreferences   # 笔刷工具枚举与偏好设置\n│   ├── InkOptionsViewController.swift   # 笔触与颜色选择浮层\n│   ├── AnnotationStore.swift            # 笔迹与锚点内存状态管理\n│   ├── BrowserChrome.swift              # 浏览器顶部导航栏与地址栏\n│   └── README.md                        # 原型专项文档\n│\n├── docs/                                # 核心技术调研与 PRD 文档\n│   ├── NoteAnywhere_MVP_PRD.md          # MVP 产品需求文档\n│   ├── Hypothesis 实现细节.md            # Hypothesis 锚定算法拆解\n│   ├── Hypothesis 锚定方案借鉴.md        # Hypothesis Selector 借鉴设计\n│   ├── W3C Web Annotation Data Model... # W3C Web Annotation 规范详解\n│   ├── WKWebView + JavaScript Bridge... # WKWebView 深入机制与 Bridge 实践\n│   ├── PKToolPicker 调研.md              # PencilKit 工具栏适配调研\n│   ├── 进度管理.md                       # 功能迭代与进度规划\n│   └── ...                              # 其他调研文档\n│\n├── implementation-notes.md              # 架构决策与实现备忘录\n└── NoteAnywhereWebPrototype.xcodeproj   # Xcode 工程文件\n```\n\n---\n\n## 📚 核心文档索引 (Documentation)\n\n| 文档名称 | 内容说明 |\n|---|---|\n| [NoteAnywhere MVP PRD](docs/NoteAnywhere_MVP_PRD.md) | 产品定位、用户流程、核心概念、文档状态流转与 MVP 范围 |\n| [W3C Web Annotation & Hypothesis 调研](docs/W3C%20Web%20Annotation%20Data%20Model%20§4.2%20Selectors%20+%20Hypothesis%20官方仓库实现.markdown) | W3C Selector 规范与开源 Hypothesis 锚定实现剖析 |\n| [Hypothesis 锚定方案借鉴](docs/Hypothesis%20锚定方案借鉴.md) | 针对移动端笔迹锚定的降级策略与匹配算法设计 |\n| [WKWebView + JS Bridge 知识指南](docs/WKWebView%20+%20JavaScript%20Bridge%20完整知识指南.md) | `WKContentWorld` 隔离、`userScript` 注入与性能优化 |\n| [PKToolPicker 调研](docs/PKToolPicker%20调研.md) | Apple PencilKit 官方工具栏集成与系统适配调研 |\n| [进度管理与��能规划](docs/进度管理.md) | 原型路线图、持久化规划与技术割舍说明 |\n\n---\n\n## 🚀 快速开始 (Quick Start)\n\n### 系统要求\n- **macOS**: 14.0+\n- **Xcode**: 15.0+ (iOS 17.0+ SDK)\n- **运行设备**: 推荐 iPad 真机（配合 Apple Pencil 体验最佳；模拟器可用鼠标模拟点击）\n\n### 构建与运行\n\n1. **克隆仓库**:\n   ```bash\n   git clone https://github.com/your-repo/NoteAnywhere.git\n   cd NoteAnywhere\n   ```\n\n2. **命令行编译（iOS 模拟器）**:\n   ```bash\n   xcodebuild \\\n     -project NoteAnywhereWebPrototype.xcodeproj \\\n     -scheme NoteAnywhereWebPrototype \\\n     -sdk iphonesimulator \\\n     -configuration Debug \\\n     CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO \\\n     build\n   ```\n\n3. **Xcode 真机部署**:\n   - 使用 Xcode 打开 `NoteAnywhereWebPrototype.xcodeproj`。\n   - 选择编译目标设备为连接的 **iPad**。\n   - 点击 `Run` (`Cmd + R`) 部署并启动 App。\n\n---\n\n## 🚧 原型限制与未来规划 (Limitations & Roadmap)\n\n### 当前原型限制 (Prototype Limitations)\n- 笔迹保存在内存中，应用退出后释放（持久化架构方案设计见 `docs/进度管理.md`）。\n- 暂未支持 `iframe`、闭合 `Shadow DOM` 以及 `Canvas / WebGL` 内部坐标锚定。\n- 若 DOM 元素尺寸发生剧烈挤压或长文重排，笔迹跟随原点平移，���会自动拉伸变形。\n\n### MVP 迭代规划 (Roadmap)\n- [ ] **L0 笔迹持久化**: Codable 序列化，一 URL 一文件存储与轻量索引。\n- [ ] **PDF & Markdown 统一转换引擎**: 网页转换 PDF 捕获、Markdown 渲染导出。\n- [ ] **扁平化 PDF 导出**: 将手写笔迹合成导出为标准 PDF 文件。\n\n---\n\n## 📄 许可证 (License)\n\n本项目采用 MIT 许可证，详情参阅 [LICENSE](LICENSE) 文件。\n"

<guidelines>
Follow these behavioral and workflow guidelines at all times:
# Documentation
- Maintain documentation integrity. Preserve all existing comments and docstrings that are unrelated to your code changes, unless the user specifies otherwise.

# Obey Explicit Directives
If the user specifies precise quantitative filtering rules, layout boundaries, or architectural preferences, enforce them exactly as requested without alteration.

# Never Guess Code Logic, Schemas, or File Paths
NEVER infer implementation details, variable names, or file locations without inspecting the authoritative source using code search and file viewing tools.

# Inspect Logs & Stack Traces Before Diagnosing Errors
NEVER form a diagnostic hypothesis for a runtime failure, or test breakage, without reading the full, un-truncated error log. When an error occurs, your VERY FIRST ACTION must be to fetch and read the exact logs. Base your diagnosis strictly on empirical log evidence.

# No Superficial Symptom Patches
NEVER resolve errors by masking symptoms, swallowing exceptions, returning dummy fallbacks, commenting out broken assertions, or deleting failing unit tests. When a test or function fails, identify why the underlying contract was broken. If an API returns missing or null data, trace the upstream data provider instead of wrapping the call in a silent try/except or returning an empty 0-byte ArrayBuffer.

# Never Declare Success Without Running Verification Commands
NEVER claim a task is resolved, a bug is fixed, or a feature is working until you have gathered concrete, empirical runtime verification demonstrating clean success. Editing a file does not equal completing the task. You MUST run the build or test command afterwards.

# Never Ignore Explicit Command Failures or Error Exit Codes
If a command fails, you MUST explicitly acknowledge the failure to the user or continue debugging. Never gloss over a build timeout or permission denied error by focusing only on the part of the code that compiled.

# Check Feature Flags & Enforce Strict Control Flow Scoping
Whenever modifying conditional branches, adding experimental features, or processing loops, ensure that new logic is strictly scoped and evaluated against all possible execution paths.

# Preserve Existing API Contracts & Avoid Unintended Side Effects
If you modify a function signature, use code search to find and update every invocation site so the parameter is actually passed.

# Silent Log Inspection & Professional Synthesis
When background tasks (run_command async, manage_task, schedule) complete or emit log notifications, inspect the log files silently. Summarize and synthesize the exact findings in clean, professional natural language.

# No Snippet Tunnel Vision
Never infer the definition of data structures (proto, struct, class, or enum schemas) from partial file views (first 15 lines or L40-L65 snippets) or design doc text.
If view_file output indicates truncation or if an imported schema is referenced, you MUST adjust StartLine/EndLine or ContentOffset to inspect the complete, exact definition of the target symbols before writing code that consumes them.

# Check Command Registries
Whenever modifying core C/C++/Java command implementations (CLIENT LIST, CLIENT KILL), explicitly search for and update corresponding command definitions across all registry files (commands.def, JSON schemas, .bzl build manifests).

# Audit Before Re-inventing
Search the codebase and recent commit history for pre-existing utility classes or decoupled architecture before writing custom helper classes from scratch.

# No Blocking Calls on Main Looper Threads
Never invoke blocking thread synchronizations (webLatch.await(500, ...), Future.get()) on main Android UI loops or single-threaded event dispatchers. 

# Thread Pool Shutdown Safety
When modifying worker thread loops or shared queues, ensure emergency stop/shutdown signals and loop termination criteria remain intact so thread join operations never deadlock.

# Exact Argument Structure
Pass arguments exactly as expected by the API (calculateRoute({ origin, destination, travelMode }) vs calculateRoute(origin, destination, travelMode)).

# Local State Mutation Only
Do not mutate private third-party DOM properties. Do not push incomplete draft objects directly into global array states; keep transient state within local component state.

# Traceback Justification Required
Every code or configuration edit during debugging MUST be justified by an explicit error traceback, log line, or verified root cause. If the root cause is unknown, investigate further before mutating code.

# Analyze Before Retrying
Never repeat the exact same broken test or shell command line with duplicate/conflicting arguments without analyzing and resolving why the previous command failed.

# Persevere on Log Extraction
If a log retrieval command fails, NEVER abandon log extraction to diagnose blindly. Immediately switch to alternative tools to inspect the actual failure traceback.

# Verify Signatures & Prop Names
Check exact variable names, component prop keys, and method signatures before passing them. Prevent NullPointerException, AttributeError, KeyError, and ReferenceError crashes by explicitly verifying object initialization and non-null states before property dereferencing (layer._path, stat.owner()).

# Dynamic Layout Math
Avoid hardcoding static pixel offsets (+ 12) or arbitrary multipliers (pill_font_size * 2.0) when computing dynamic UI layout heights; calculate exact container bounds from wrapped elements.

</guidelines>

MVP 目标\n24\t\n25\t1. 支持用户从系统分享入口快速导入 PDF、Markdown 和网页。\n26\t2. 将不同来源的内容统一转换为可手写批注的 PDF 文档。\n27\t3. 提供接近 Apple 系统绘图工具体验的基础手写批注能力。\n28\t4. 自动、可靠地保存批注，并允许用户导出包含批注的 PDF。\n29\t5. 在文件列表中按照最后活动时间快速找到最近处理的文档。\n30\t\n31\t## 3. 非目标\n32\t\n33\t以下能力不属于 MVP：\n34\t\n35\t- Markdown 编辑与原文管理。\n36\t- Markdown 或网页内容变化后的自动同步。\n37\t- PDF 文本高亮、下划线、删除线、文本备注等语义批注。\n38\t- OCR 与扫描 PDF 文字识别。\n39\t- 网页像素级还原。\n40\t- 网页批注与最新网页内容自动对齐。\n41\t- 多人协作、评论与分享链接。\n42\t- 账号系统与跨设备同步。\n43\t- 文件夹、标签等复杂文档组织能力。\n44\t- 导出后仍可在第三方 PDF 工具中编辑的标准 PDF Annotation。\n45\t\n46\t## 4. 核心概念\n47\t\n48\t### 4.1 文档来源类型\n49\t\n50\t- PDF\n51\t- Markdown\n52\t- Web\n53\t\n54\t### 4.2 文档状态\n55\t\n56\t| 状态 | 含义 | 点击行为 |\n57\t|---|---|---|\n58\t| importing | 正在复制导入内容 | 展示进度，不进入批注页 |\n59\t| converting | Markdown 或网页正在生成 PDF | 展示转换状态 |\n60\t| webReady | 网页已保存 URL，尚未生成 PDF | 打开内置浏览器 |\n61\t| annotatable | PDF 已准备完成 | 进入批注模式 |\n62\t| failed | 导入或转换失败 | 展示失败原因和重试入口 |\n63\t| corrupted | PDF 无法读取或文件损坏 | 展示错误，并提供删除或重新导入提示 |\n64\t\n65\t### 4.3 最后活动时间\n66\t\n67\t以下操作更新最后活动时间：\n68\t\n69\t- 文件成功导入。\n70\t- Markdown 或网页成功生成 PDF。\n71\t- 用户新增、修改或删除批注。\n72\t- 用户重命名文档。\n73\t\n74\t以下操作不更新最后活动时间：\n75\t\n76\t- 仅打开文档、翻页、滚动或缩放。\n77\t- 导出或分享文档。\n78\t- 后台自动保存。\n79\t- 后台生成缩略图。\n80\t\n81\t## 5. 用户入口与核心流程\n82\t\n83\t### 5.1 App 主入口\n84\t\n85\t1. 用户打开 NoteAnywhere。\n86\t2. App 展示文件列表。\n87\t3. 文件默认按照最后活动时间倒序排列。\n88\t4. 用户点击可批注文档，进入批注模式。\n89\t5. 用户点击尚未捕获的网页，进入内置浏览器。\n90\t6. 用户点击失败条目，查看失败原因并重试。\n91\t\n92\t### 5.2 分享入口\n93\t\n94\tNoteAnywhere 作为系统分享目标，支持接收：\n95\t\n96\t- `.pdf`\n97\t- `.md`\n98\t- `.markdown`\n99\t- Safari 分享的 HTTP/HTTPS URL\n100\t"

  "CodeContent": "# NoteAnywhere\n\n<p align=\"center\">\n  <b>以 PDF 为统一载体、支持网页/Markdown/PDF 交互与 Apple Pencil 手写批注的 iOS / iPadOS 应用</b>\n</p>\n\n---\n\n## 📌 项目概述 (Overview)\n\n**NoteAnywhere** 是一款专注于阅读与高自由度手写批注的 iOS/iPadOS 应用（优先针对 iPad 与 Apple Pencil 深度优化）。\n\n项目旨在打破网页、文档、PDF 之间的媒介壁垒：\n- **PDF**: 从系统分享或文件导入后直接进入批注模式。\n- **Markdown**: 动态渲染并转化为 PDF 快照后提供手写批注。\n- **Web 网页**: 在内置浏览器中实时交互（展开折叠、切页、点击），并支持** Apple Pencil 在动态 DOM 元素上进行实时手写锚定批注**。\n\n目前项目包含完整的产品需求文档（MVP PRD）与高完成度的技术验证原型 **`NoteAnywhereWebPrototype`**。\n\n---\n\n## ✨ 原型核心特性 (Prototype Features)\n\n当前原型项目 `NoteAnywhereWebPrototype` 成功验证了 **“可交互网页 + Apple Pencil DOM 锚定笔迹”** 的核心难题：\n\n1. **手触与笔触解耦 (Dual-Input Coexistence)**\n   - 自定义 `PencilStrokeGestureRecognizer` 仅捕获 Apple Pencil 触摸事件，完全不拦截手指事件。\n   - 手指可流畅滚动、点击、双指缩放（Pinch-Zoom）网页，Apple Pencil 随时落笔书写。\n   - 接入 Apple Pencil `predictedTouches` API，极大降低渲染与跟手延迟。\n\n2. **DOM 实时锚定与跟随 (DOM Anchoring & Transform Tracking)**\n   - 落笔瞬间触发 `document.elementFromPoint` 发起 DOM 锚定，与笔迹采集并行执行。\n   - 笔迹存储为相对于锚点 DOM 元素的相对 CSS 偏移坐标。\n   - 页面滚动与缩放时通过 `CAShapeLayer` / `CALayer` 的 `transform` 实现帧级跟随，零卡顿重绘。\n\n3. **W3C 标准 Selector 容错匹配链 (W3C Selector & Resilient Recovery)**\n   - 锚点序列化遵循 W3C Web Annotation 标准（`CssSelector` + `TextQuoteSelector`）。\n   - 页面 DOM 结构变动时按降级链恢复：`结构定位` ➔ `局部文本搜索` ➔ `全文文本搜索` ➔ `孤儿批注`（隐藏但完整保留数据）。\n\n4. **局部精准监听与沙盒隔离 (Scoped Observer & JS Isolation)**\n   - `MutationObserver` 与 `ResizeObserver` 仅监听已锚定元素及其祖先链，无笔迹时不占用 CPU/内存资源。\n   - 所有 JavaScript 桥接代码均注入在 `WKContentWorld.defaultClient` 隔离世界，防止网页脚本探测与覆盖。\n\n5. **SPA 导航与状态管理 (SPA Navigation & State Handling)**\n   - 通过 `WKWebView.url` 观察及 pushState/replaceState KVO 识别 SPA 单页应用导航。\n   - 切页时自动收起旧页笔迹并释放 JS 侧锚点，返回时重新恢复。\n\n---\n\n## 🛠 架构设计与源码结构 (Architecture)\n\n```\nNoteAnywhere/\n├── NoteAnywhereWebPrototype/            # iOS App 原型核心源码\n│   ├── NoteAnywhereWebPrototypeApp.swift  # SwiftUI 应用入口\n│   ├── HomeView.swift                   # 首页（批注记录列表与快捷测试入口）\n│   ├── WebAnnotationViewController.swift # 核心视图控制器 (UIKit + WKWebView + Pencil Overlay)\n│   ├── InkOverlayView.swift             # 笔迹绘制覆盖层 (CAShapeLayer / CALayer)\n│   ├── PencilStrokeGestureRecognizer.swift # 独立 Pencil 手势识别器\n│   ├── PencilKitInk.swift / SelfCheck   # PencilKit 墨迹集成与自检\n│   ├── InkTool.swift / InkPreferences   # 笔刷工具枚举与偏好设置\n│   ├── InkOptionsViewController.swift   # 笔触与颜色选择浮层\n│   ├── AnnotationStore.swift            # 笔迹与锚点内存状态管理\n│   ├── BrowserChrome.swift              # 浏览器顶部导航栏与地址栏\n│   └── README.md                        # 原型专项文档\n│\n├── docs/                                # 核心技术调研与 PRD 文档\n│   ├── NoteAnywhere_MVP_PRD.md          # MVP 产品需求文档\n│   ├── Hypothesis 实现细节.md            # Hypothesis 锚定算法拆解\n│   ├── Hypothesis 锚定方案借鉴.md        # Hypothesis Selector 借鉴设计\n│   ├── W3C Web Annotation Data Model... # W3C Web Annotation 规范详解\n│   ├── WKWebView + JavaScript Bridge... # WKWebView 深入机制与 Bridge 实践\n│   ├── PKToolPicker 调研.md              # PencilKit 工具栏适配调研\n│   ├── 进度管理.md                       # 功能迭代与进度规划\n│   └── ...                              # 其他调研文档\n│\n├── implementation-notes.md              # 架构决策与实现备忘录\n└── NoteAnywhereWebPrototype.xcodeproj   # Xcode 工程文件\n```\n\n---\n\n## 📚 核心文档索引 (Documentation)\n\n| 文档名称 | 内容说明 |\n|---|---|\n| [NoteAnywhere MVP PRD](docs/NoteAnywhere_MVP_PRD.md) | 产品定位、用户流程、核心概念、文档状态流转与 MVP 范围 |\n| [W3C Web Annotation & Hypothesis 调研](docs/W3C%20Web%20Annotation%20Data%20Model%20§4.2%20Selectors%20+%20Hypothesis%20官方仓库实现.markdown) | W3C Selector 规范与开源 Hypothesis 锚定实现剖析 |\n| [Hypothesis 锚定方案借鉴](docs/Hypothesis%20锚定方案借鉴.md) | 针对移动端笔迹锚定的降级策略与匹配算法设计 |\n| [WKWebView + JS Bridge 知识指南](docs/WKWebView%20+%20JavaScript%20Bridge%20完整知识指南.md) | `WKContentWorld` 隔离、`userScript` 注入与性能优化 |\n| [PKToolPicker 调研](docs/PKToolPicker%20调研.md) | Apple PencilKit 官方工具栏集成与系统适配调研 |\n| [进度管理与��能规划](docs/进度管理.md) | 原型路线图、持久化规划与技术割舍说明 |\n\n---\n\n## 🚀 快速开始 (Quick Start)\n\n### 系统要求\n- **macOS**: 14.0+\n- **Xcode**: 15.0+ (iOS 17.0+ SDK)\n- **运行设备**: 推荐 iPad 真机（配合 Apple Pencil 体验最佳；模拟器可用鼠标模拟点击）\n\n### 构建与运行\n\n1. **克隆仓库**:\n   ```bash\n   git clone https://github.com/your-repo/NoteAnywhere.git\n   cd NoteAnywhere\n   ```\n\n2. **命令行编译（iOS 模拟器）**:\n   ```bash\n   xcodebuild \\\n     -project NoteAnywhereWebPrototype.xcodeproj \\\n     -scheme NoteAnywhereWebPrototype \\\n     -sdk iphonesimulator \\\n     -configuration Debug \\\n     CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO \\\n     build\n   ```\n\n3. **Xcode 真机部署**:\n   - 使用 Xcode 打开 `NoteAnywhereWebPrototype.xcodeproj`。\n   - 选择编译目标设备为连接的 **iPad**。\n   - 点击 `Run` (`Cmd + R`) 部署并启动 App。\n\n---\n\n## 🚧 原型限制与未来规划 (Limitations & Roadmap)\n\n### 当前原型限制 (Prototype Limitations)\n- 笔迹保存在内存中，应用退出后释放（持久化架构方案设计见 `docs/进度管理.md`）。\n- 暂未支持 `iframe`、闭合 `Shadow DOM` 以及 `Canvas / WebGL` 内部坐标锚定。\n- 若 DOM 元素尺寸发生剧烈挤压或长文重排，笔迹跟随原点平移，���会自动拉伸变形。\n\n### MVP 迭代规划 (Roadmap)\n- [ ] **L0 笔迹持久化**: Codable 序列化，一 URL 一文件存储与轻量索引。\n- [ ] **PDF & Markdown 统一转换引擎**: 网页转换 PDF 捕获、Markdown 渲染导出。\n- [ ] **扁平化 PDF 导出**: 将手写笔迹合成导出为标准 PDF 文件。\n\n---\n\n## 📄 许可证 (License)\n\n本项目采用 MIT 许可证，详情参阅 [LICENSE](LICENSE) 文件。\n"

- **scripts/** - Helper scripts and utilities that extend your capabilities

- **references/** - Contains additional documentation that agents can read when needed

<guidelines>
Follow these behavioral and workflow guidelines at all times:
# Documentation
- Maintain documentation integrity. Preserve all existing comments and docstrings that are unrelated to your code changes, unless the user specifies otherwise.

# Obey Explicit Directives
If the user specifies precise quantitative filtering rules, layout boundaries, or architectural preferences, enforce them exactly as requested without alteration.

# Never Guess Code Logic, Schemas, or File Paths
NEVER infer implementation details, variable names, or file locations without inspecting the authoritative source using code search and file viewing tools.

# Inspect Logs & Stack Traces Before Diagnosing Errors
NEVER form a diagnostic hypothesis for a runtime failure, or test breakage, without reading the full, un-truncated error log. When an error occurs, your VERY FIRST ACTION must be to fetch and read the exact logs. Base your diagnosis strictly on empirical log evidence.

# No Superficial Symptom Patches
NEVER resolve errors by masking symptoms, swallowing exceptions, returning dummy fallbacks, commenting out broken assertions, or deleting failing unit tests. When a test or function fails, identify why the underlying contract was broken. If an API returns missing or null data, trace the upstream data provider instead of wrapping the call in a silent try/except or returning an empty 0-byte ArrayBuffer.

# Never Declare Success Without Running Verification Commands
NEVER claim a task is resolved, a bug is fixed, or a feature is working until you have gathered concrete, empirical runtime verification demonstrating clean success. Editing a file does not equal completing the task. You MUST run the build or test command afterwards.

# Never Ignore Explicit Command Failures or Error Exit Codes
If a command fails, you MUST explicitly acknowledge the failure to the user or continue debugging. Never gloss over a build timeout or permission denied error by focusing only on the part of the code that compiled.

# Check Feature Flags & Enforce Strict Control Flow Scoping
Whenever modifying conditional branches, adding experimental features, or processing loops, ensure that new logic is strictly scoped and evaluated against all possible execution paths.

# Preserve Existing API Contracts & Avoid Unintended Side Effects
If you modify a function signature, use code search to find and update every invocation site so the parameter is actually passed.

# Silent Log Inspection & Professional Synthesis
When background tasks (run_command async, manage_task, schedule) complete or emit log notifications, inspect the log files silently. Summarize and synthesize the exact findings in clean, professional natural language.

# No Snippet Tunnel Vision
Never infer the definition of data structures (proto, struct, class, or enum schemas) from partial file views (first 15 lines or L40-L65 snippets) or design doc text.
If view_file output indicates truncation or if an imported schema is referenced, you MUST adjust StartLine/EndLine or ContentOffset to inspect the complete, exact definition of the target symbols before writing code that consumes them.

# Check Command Registries
Whenever modifying core C/C++/Java command implementations (CLIENT LIST, CLIENT KILL), explicitly search for and update corresponding command definitions across all registry files (commands.def, JSON schemas, .bzl build manifests).

# Audit Before Re-inventing
Search the codebase and recent commit history for pre-existing utility classes or decoupled architecture before writing custom helper classes from scratch.

# No Blocking Calls on Main Looper Threads
Never invoke blocking thread synchronizations (webLatch.await(500, ...), Future.get()) on main Android UI loops or single-threaded event dispatchers. 

# Thread Pool Shutdown Safety
When modifying worker thread loops or shared queues, ensure emergency stop/shutdown signals and loop termination criteria remain intact so thread join operations never deadlock.

# Exact Argument Structure
Pass arguments exactly as expected by the API (calculateRoute({ origin, destination, travelMode }) vs calculateRoute(origin, destination, travelMode)).

# Local State Mutation Only
Do not mutate private third-party DOM properties. Do not push incomplete draft objects directly into global array states; keep transient state within local component state.

# Traceback Justification Required
Every code or configuration edit during debugging MUST be justified by an explicit error traceback, log line, or verified root cause. If the root cause is unknown, investigate further before mutating code.

# Analyze Before Retrying
Never repeat the exact same broken test or shell command line with duplicate/conflicting arguments without analyzing and resolving why the previous command failed.

# Persevere on Log Extraction
If a log retrieval command fails, NEVER abandon log extraction to diagnose blindly. Immediately switch to alternative tools to inspect the actual failure traceback.

# Verify Signatures & Prop Names
Check exact variable names, component prop keys, and method signatures before passing them. Prevent NullPointerException, AttributeError, KeyError, and ReferenceError crashes by explicitly verifying object initialization and non-null states before property dereferencing (layer._path, stat.owner()).

# Dynamic Layout Math
Avoid hardcoding static pixel offsets (+ 12) or arbitrary multipliers (pill_font_size * 2.0) when computing dynamic UI layout heights; calculate exact container bounds from wrapped elements.

</guidelines>

  "TargetFile": "/Users/cddchen/Documents/NoteAnywhere/README.md",

  "toolSummary": "Create root README.md file",
  "Description": "创建根目录 README.md 文档",
  "CodeContent": "# NoteAnywhere\n\n<p align=\"center\">\n  <b>以 PDF 为统一载体、支持网页/Markdown/PDF 交互与 Apple Pencil 手写批注的 iOS / iPadOS 应用</b>\n</p>\n\n---\n\n## 📌 项目概述 (Overview)\n\n**NoteAnywhere** 是一款专注于阅读与高自由度手写批注的 iOS/iPadOS 应用（优先针对 iPad 与 Apple Pencil 深度优化）。\n\n项目旨在打破网页、文档、PDF 之间的媒介壁垒：\n- **PDF**: 从系统分享或文件导入后直接进入批注模式。\n- **Markdown**: 动态渲染并转化为 PDF 快照后提供手写批注。\n- **Web 网页**: 在内置浏览器中实时交互（展开折叠、切页、点击），并支持** Apple Pencil 在动态 DOM 元素上进行实时手写锚定批注**。\n\n目前项目包含完整的产品需求文档（MVP PRD）与高完成度的技术验证原型 **`NoteAnywhereWebPrototype`**。\n\n---\n\n## ✨ 原型核心特性 (Prototype Features)\n\n当前原型项目 `NoteAnywhereWebPrototype` 成功验证了 **“可交互网页 + Apple Pencil DOM 锚定笔迹”** 的核心难题：\n\n1. **手触与笔触解耦 (Dual-Input Coexistence)**\n   - 自定义 `PencilStrokeGestureRecognizer` 仅捕获 Apple Pencil 触摸事件，完全不拦截手指事件。\n   - 手指可流畅滚动、点击、双指缩放（Pinch-Zoom）网页，Apple Pencil 随时落笔书写。\n   - 接入 Apple Pencil `predictedTouches` API，极大降低渲染与跟手延迟。\n\n2. **DOM 实时锚定与跟随 (DOM Anchoring & Transform Tracking)**\n   - 落笔瞬间触发 `document.elementFromPoint` 发起 DOM 锚定，与笔迹采集并行执行。\n   - 笔迹存储为相对于锚点 DOM 元素的相对 CSS 偏移坐标。\n   - 页面滚动与缩放时通过 `CAShapeLayer` / `CALayer` 的 `transform` 实现帧级跟随，零卡顿重绘。\n\n3. **W3C 标准 Selector 容错匹配链 (W3C Selector & Resilient Recovery)**\n   - 锚点序列化遵循 W3C Web Annotation 标准（`CssSelector` + `TextQuoteSelector`）。\n   - 页面 DOM 结构变动时按降级链恢复：`结构定位` ➔ `局部文本搜索` ➔ `全文文本搜索` ➔ `孤儿批注`（隐藏但完整保留数据）。\n\n4. **局部精准监听与沙盒隔离 (Scoped Observer & JS Isolation)**\n   - `MutationObserver` 与 `ResizeObserver` 仅监听已锚定元素及其祖先链，无笔迹时不占用 CPU/内存资源。\n   - 所有 JavaScript 桥接代码均注入在 `WKContentWorld.defaultClient` 隔离世界，防止网页脚本探测与覆盖。\n\n5. **SPA 导航与状态管理 (SPA Navigation & State Handling)**\n   - 通过 `WKWebView.url` 观察及 pushState/replaceState KVO 识别 SPA 单页应用导航。\n   - 切页时自动收起旧页笔迹并释放 JS 侧锚点，返回时重新恢复。\n\n---\n\n## 🛠 架构设计与源码结构 (Architecture)\n\n```\nNoteAnywhere/\n├── NoteAnywhereWebPrototype/            # iOS App 原型核心源码\n│   ├── NoteAnywhereWebPrototypeApp.swift  # SwiftUI 应用入口\n│   ├── HomeView.swift                   # 首页（批注记录列表与快捷测试入口）\n│   ├── WebAnnotationViewController.swift # 核心视图控制器 (UIKit + WKWebView + Pencil Overlay)\n│   ├── InkOverlayView.swift             # 笔迹绘制覆盖层 (CAShapeLayer / CALayer)\n│   ├── PencilStrokeGestureRecognizer.swift # 独立 Pencil 手势识别器\n│   ├── PencilKitInk.swift / SelfCheck   # PencilKit 墨迹集成与自检\n│   ├── InkTool.swift / InkPreferences   # 笔刷工具枚举与偏好设置\n│   ├── InkOptionsViewController.swift   # 笔触与颜色选择浮层\n│   ├── AnnotationStore.swift            # 笔迹与锚点内存状态管理\n│   ├── BrowserChrome.swift              # 浏览器顶部导航栏与地址栏\n│   └── README.md                        # 原型专项文档\n│\n├── docs/                                # 核心技术调研与 PRD 文档\n│   ├── NoteAnywhere_MVP_PRD.md          # MVP 产品需求文档\n│   ├── Hypothesis 实现细节.md            # Hypothesis 锚定算法拆解\n│   ├── Hypothesis 锚定方案借鉴.md        # <guidelines>
Follow these behavioral and workflow guidelines at all times:
# Documentation
- Maintain documentation integrity. Preserve all existing comments and docstrings that are unrelated to your code changes, unless the user specifies otherwise.

# Obey Explicit Directives
If the user specifies precise quantitative filtering rules, layout boundaries, or architectural preferences, enforce them exactly as requested without alteration.

# Never Guess Code Logic, Schemas, or File Paths
NEVER infer implementation details, variable names, or file locations without inspecting the authoritative source using code search and file viewing tools.

# Inspect Logs & Stack Traces Before Diagnosing Errors
NEVER form a diagnostic hypothesis for a runtime failure, or test breakage, without reading the full, un-truncated error log. When an error occurs, your VERY FIRST ACTION must be to fetch and read the exact logs. Base your diagnosis strictly on empirical log evidence.

# No Superficial Symptom Patches
NEVER resolve errors by masking symptoms, swallowing exceptions, returning dummy fallbacks, commenting out broken assertions, or deleting failing unit tests. When a test or function fails, identify why the underlying contract was broken. If an API returns missing or null data, trace the upstream data provider instead of wrapping the call in a silent try/except or returning an empty 0-byte ArrayBuffer.

# Never Declare Success Without Running Verification Commands
NEVER claim a task is resolved, a bug is fixed, or a feature is working until you have gathered concrete, empirical runtime verification demonstrating clean success. Editing a file does not equal completing the task. You MUST run the build or test command afterwards.

# Never Ignore Explicit Command Failures or Error Exit Codes
If a command fails, you MUST explicitly acknowledge the failure to the user or continue debugging. Never gloss over a build timeout or permission denied error by focusing only on the part of the code that compiled.

# Check Feature Flags & Enforce Strict Control Flow Scoping
Whenever modifying conditional branches, adding experimental features, or processing loops, ensure that new logic is strictly scoped and evaluated against all possible execution paths.

# Preserve Existing API Contracts & Avoid Unintended Side Effects
If you modify a function signature, use code search to find and update every invocation site so the parameter is actually passed.

# Silent Log Inspection & Professional Synthesis
When background tasks (run_command async, manage_task, schedule) complete or emit log notifications, inspect the log files silently. Summarize and synthesize the exact findings in clean, professional natural language.

# No Snippet Tunnel Vision
Never infer the definition of data structures (proto, struct, class, or enum schemas) from partial file views (first 15 lines or L40-L65 snippets) or design doc text.
If view_file output indicates truncation or if an imported schema is referenced, you MUST adjust StartLine/EndLine or ContentOffset to inspect the complete, exact definition of the target symbols before writing code that consumes them.

# Check Command Registries
Whenever modifying core C/C++/Java command implementations (CLIENT LIST, CLIENT KILL), explicitly search for and update corresponding command definitions across all registry files (commands.def, JSON schemas, .bzl build manifests).

# Audit Before Re-inventing
Search the codebase and recent commit history for pre-existing utility classes or decoupled architecture before writing custom helper classes from scratch.

# No Blocking Calls on Main Looper Threads
Never invoke blocking thread synchronizations (webLatch.await(500, ...), Future.get()) on main Android UI loops or single-threaded event dispatchers. 

# Thread Pool Shutdown Safety
When modifying worker thread loops or shared queues, ensure emergency stop/shutdown signals and loop termination criteria remain intact so thread join operations never deadlock.

# Exact Argument Structure
Pass arguments exactly as expected by the API (calculateRoute({ origin, destination, travelMode }) vs calculateRoute(origin, destination, travelMode)).

# Local State Mutation Only
Do not mutate private third-party DOM properties. Do not push incomplete draft objects directly into global array states; keep transient state within local component state.

# Traceback Justification Required
Every code or configuration edit during debugging MUST be justified by an explicit error traceback, log line, or verified root cause. If the root cause is unknown, investigate further before mutating code.

# Analyze Before Retrying
Never repeat the exact same broken test or shell command line with duplicate/conflicting arguments without analyzing and resolving why the previous command failed.

# Persevere on Log Extraction
If a log retrieval command fails, NEVER abandon log extraction to diagnose blindly. Immediately switch to alternative tools to inspect the actual failure traceback.

# Verify Signatures & Prop Names
Check exact variable names, component prop keys, and method signatures before passing them. Prevent NullPointerException, AttributeError, KeyError, and ReferenceError crashes by explicitly verifying object initialization and non-null states before property dereferencing (layer._path, stat.owner()).

# Dynamic Layout Math
Avoid hardcoding static pixel offsets (+ 12) or arbitrary multipliers (pill_font_size * 2.0) when computing dynamic UI layout heights; calculate exact container bounds from wrapped elements.

MVP 目标\n24\t\n25\t1. 支持用户从系统分享入口快速导入 PDF、Markdown 和网页。\n26\t2. 将不同来源的内容统一转换为可手写批注的 PDF 文档。\n27\t3. 提供接近 Apple 系统绘图工具体验的基础手写批注能力。\n28\t4. 自动、可靠地保存批注，并允许用户导出包含批注的 PDF。\n29\t5. 在文件列表中按照最后活动时间快速找到最近处理的文档。\n30\t\n31\t## 3. 非目标\n32\t\n33\t以下能力不属于 MVP：\n34\t\n35\t- Markdown 编辑与原文管理。\n36\t- Markdown 或网页内容变化后的自动同步。\n37\t- PDF 文本高亮、下划线、删除线、文本备注等语义批注。\n38\t- OCR 与扫描 PDF 文字识别。\n39\t- 网页像素级还原。\n40\t- 网页批注与最新网页内容自动对齐。\n41\t- 多人协作、评论与分享链接。\n42\t- 账号系统与跨设备同步。\n43\t- 文件夹、标签等复杂文档组织能力。\n44\t- 导出后仍可在第三方 PDF 工具中编辑的标准 PDF Annotation。\n45\t\n46\t## 4. 核心概念\n47\t\n48\t### 4.1 文档来源类型\n49\t\n50\t- PDF\n51\t- Markdown\n52\t- Web\n53\t\n54\t### 4.2 文档状态\n55\t\n56\t| 状态 | 含义 | 点击行为 |\n57\t|---|---|---|\n58\t| importing | 正在复制导入内容 | 展示进度，不进入批注页 |\n59\t| converting | Markdown 或网页正在生成 PDF | 展示转换状态 |\n60\t| webReady | 网页已保存 URL，尚未生成 PDF | 打开内置浏览器 |\n61\t| annotatable | PDF 已准备完成 | 进入批注模式 |\n62\t| failed | 导入或转换失败 | 展示失败原因和重试入口 |\n63\t| corrupted | PDF 无法读取或文件损坏 | 展示错误，并提供删除或重新导入提示 |\n64\t\n65\t### 4.3 最后活动时间\n66\t\n67\t以下操作更新最后活动时间：\n68\t\n69\t- 文件成功导入。\n70\t- Markdown 或网页成功生成 PDF。\n71\t- 用户新增、修改或删除批注。\n72\t- 用户重命名文档。\n73\t\n74\t以下操作不更新最后活动时间：\n75\t\n76\t- 仅打开文档、翻页、滚动或缩放。\n77\t- 导出或分享文档。\n78\t- 后台自动保存。\n79\t- 后台生成缩略图。\n80\t\n81\t## 5. 用户入口与核心流程\n82\t\n83\t### 5.1 App 主入口\n84\t\n85\t1. 用户打开 NoteAnywhere。\n86\t2.   "CodeContent": "# NoteAnywhere\n\n<p align=\"center\">\n  <b>以 PDF 为统一载体、支持网页/Markdown/PDF 交互与 Apple Pencil 手写批注的 iOS / iPadOS 应用</b>\n</p>\n\n---\n\n## 📌 项目概述 (Overview)\n\n**NoteAnywhere** 是一款专注于阅读与高自由度手写批注的 iOS/iPadOS 应用（优先针对 iPad 与 Apple Pencil 深度优化）。\n\n项目旨在打破网页、文档、PDF 之间的媒介壁垒：\n- **PDF**: 从系统分享或文件导入后直接进入批注模式。\n- **Markdown**: 动态渲染并转化为 PDF 快照后提供手写批注。\n- **Web 网页**: 在内置浏览器中实时交互（展开折叠、切页、点击），并支持** Apple Pencil 在动态 DOM 元素上进行实时手写锚定批注**。\n\n目前项目包含完整的产品需求文档（MVP PRD）与高完成度的技术验证原型 **`NoteAnywhereWebPrototype`**。\n\n---\n\n## ✨ 原型核心特性 (Prototype Features)\n\n当前原型项目 `NoteAnywhereWebPrototype` 成功验证了 **“可交互网页 + Apple Pencil DOM 锚定笔迹”** 的核心难题：\n\n1. **手触与笔触解耦 (Dual-Input Coexistence)**\n   - 自定义 `PencilStrokeGestureRecognizer` 仅捕获 Apple Pencil 触摸事件，完全不拦截手指事件。\n   - 手指可流畅滚动、点击、双指缩放（Pinch-Zoom）网页，Apple Pencil 随时落笔书写。\n   - 接入 Apple Pencil `predictedTouches` API，极大降低渲染与跟手延迟。\n\n2. **DOM 实时锚定与跟随 (DOM Anchoring & Transform Tracking)**\n   - 落笔瞬间触发 `document.elementFromPoint` 发起 DOM 锚定，与笔迹采集并行执行。\n   - 笔迹存储为相对于锚点 DOM 元素的相对 CSS 偏移坐标。\n   - 页面滚动与缩放时通过 `CAShapeLayer` / `CALayer` 的 `transform` 实现帧级跟随，零卡顿重绘。\n\n3. **W3C 标准 Selector 容错匹配链 (W3C Selector & Resilient Recovery)**\n   - 锚点序列化遵循 W3C Web Annotation 标准（`CssSelector` + `TextQuoteSelector`）。\n   - 页面 DOM 结构变动时按降级链恢复：`结构定位` ➔ `局部文本搜索` ➔ `全文文本搜索` ➔ `孤儿批注`（隐藏但完整保留数据）。\n\n4. **局部精准监听与沙盒隔离 (Scoped Observer & JS Isolation)**\n   - `MutationObserver` 与 `ResizeObserver` 仅监听已锚定元素及其祖先链，无笔迹时不占用 CPU/内存资源。\n   - 所有 JavaScript 桥接代码均注入在 `WKContentWorld.defaultClient` 隔离世界，防止网页脚本探测与覆盖。\n\n5. **SPA 导航与状态管理 (SPA Navigation & State Handling)**\n   - 通过 `WKWebView.url` 观察及 pushState/replaceState KVO 识别 SPA 单页应用导航。\n   - 切页时自动收起旧页笔迹并释放 JS 侧锚点，返回时重新恢复。\n\n---\n\n## 🛠 架构设计与源码结构 (Architecture)\n\n```\nNoteAnywhere/\n├── NoteAnywhereWebPrototype/            # iOS App 原型核心源码\n│   ├── NoteAnywhereWebPrototypeApp.swift  # SwiftUI 应用入口\n│   ├── HomeView.swift                   # 首页（批注记录列表与快捷测试入口）\n│   ├── WebAnnotationViewController.swift # 核心视图控制器 (UIKit + WKWebView + Pencil Overlay)\n│   ├── InkOverlayView.swift             # 笔迹绘制覆盖层 (CAShapeLayer / CALayer)\n│   ├── PencilStrokeGestureRecognizer.swift # 独立 Pencil 手势识别器\n│   ├── PencilKitInk.swift / SelfCheck   # PencilKit 墨迹集成与自检\n│   ├── InkTool.swift / InkPreferences   # 笔刷工具枚举与偏好设置\n│   ├── InkOptionsViewController.swift   # 笔触与颜色选择浮层\n│   ├── AnnotationStore.swift            # 笔迹与锚点内存状态管理\n│   ├── BrowserChrome.swift              # 浏览器顶部导航栏与地址栏\n│   └── README.md                        # 原型专项文档\n│\n├── docs/                                # 核心技术调研与 PRD 文档\n│   ├── NoteAnywhere_MVP_PRD.md          # MVP 产品需求文档\n│   ├── Hypothesis 实现细节.md            # Hypothesis 锚定算法拆解\n│   ├── Hypothesis 锚定方案借鉴.md        # Hypothesis Selector 借鉴设计\n│   ├── W3C Web Annotation Data Model... # W3C Web Annotation 规范详解\n│   ├── WKWebView + JavaScript Bridge... # WKWebView 深入机制与 Bridge 实践\n│   ├── PKToolPicker 调研.md              # PencilKit 工具栏适配调研\n│   ├── 进度管理.md                       # 功能迭代与进度规划\n│   └── ...                              # 其他调研文档\n│\n├── implementation-notes.md              # 架构决策与实现备忘录\n└── NoteAnywhereWebPrototype.xcodeproj   # <guidelines>
Follow these behavioral and workflow guidelines at all times:
# Documentation
- Maintain documentation integrity. Preserve all existing comments and docstrings that are unrelated to your code changes, unless the user specifies otherwise.

# Obey Explicit Directives
If the user specifies precise quantitative filtering rules, layout boundaries, or architectural preferences, enforce them exactly as requested without alteration.

# Never Guess Code Logic, Schemas, or File Paths
NEVER infer implementation details, variable names, or file locations without inspecting the authoritative source using code search and file viewing tools.

# Inspect Logs & Stack Traces Before Diagnosing Errors
NEVER form a diagnostic hypothesis for a runtime failure, or test breakage, without reading the full, un-truncated error log. When an error occurs, your VERY FIRST ACTION must be to fetch and read the exact logs. Base your diagnosis strictly on empirical log evidence.

# No Superficial Symptom Patches
NEVER resolve errors by masking symptoms, swallowing exceptions, returning dummy fallbacks, commenting out broken assertions, or deleting failing unit tests. When a test or function fails, identify why the underlying contract was broken. If an API returns missing or null data, trace the upstream data provider instead of wrapping the call in a silent try/except or returning an empty 0-byte ArrayBuffer.

# Never Declare Success Without Running Verification Commands
NEVER claim a task is resolved, a bug is fixed, or a feature is working until you have gathered concrete, empirical runtime verification demonstrating clean success. Editing a file does not equal completing the task. You MUST run the build or test command afterwards.

# Never Ignore Explicit Command Failures or Error Exit Codes
If a command fails, you MUST explicitly acknowledge the failure to the user or continue debugging. Never gloss over a build timeout or permission denied error by focusing only on the part of the code that compiled.

# Check Feature Flags & Enforce Strict Control Flow Scoping
Whenever modifying conditional branches, adding experimental features, or processing loops, ensure that new logic is strictly scoped and evaluated against all possible execution paths.

# Preserve Existing API Contracts & Avoid Unintended Side Effects
If you modify a function signature, use code search to find and update every invocation site so the parameter is actually passed.

# Silent Log Inspection & Professional Synthesis
When background tasks (run_command async, manage_task, schedule) complete or emit log notifications, inspect the log files silently. Summarize and synthesize the exact findings in clean, professional natural language.

# No Snippet Tunnel Vision
Never infer the definition of data structures (proto, struct, class, or enum schemas) from partial file views (first 15 lines or L40-L65 snippets) or design doc text.
If view_file output indicates truncation or if an imported schema is referenced, you MUST adjust StartLine/EndLine or ContentOffset to inspect the complete, exact definition of the target symbols before writing code that consumes them.

# Check Command Registries
Whenever modifying core C/C++/Java command implementations (CLIENT LIST, CLIENT KILL), explicitly search for and update corresponding command definitions across all registry files (commands.def, JSON schemas, .bzl build manifests).

# Audit Before Re-inventing
Search the codebase and recent commit history for pre-existing utility classes or decoupled architecture before writing custom helper classes from scratch.

# No Blocking Calls on Main Looper Threads
Never invoke blocking thread synchronizations (webLatch.await(500, ...), Future.get()) on main Android UI loops or single-threaded event dispatchers. 

# Thread Pool Shutdown Safety
When modifying worker thread loops or shared queues, ensure emergency stop/shutdown signals and loop termination criteria remain intact so thread join operations never deadlock.

# Exact Argument Structure
Pass arguments exactly as expected by the API (calculateRoute({ origin, destination, travelMode }) vs calculateRoute(origin, destination, travelMode)).

# Local State Mutation Only
Do not mutate private third-party DOM properties. Do not push incomplete draft objects directly into global array states; keep transient state within local component state.

# Traceback Justification Required
Every code or configuration edit during debugging MUST be justified by an explicit error traceback, log line, or verified root cause. If the root cause is unknown, investigate further before mutating code.

# Analyze Before Retrying
Never repeat the exact same broken test or shell command line with duplicate/conflicting arguments without analyzing and resolving why the previous command failed.

# Persevere on Log Extraction
If a log retrieval command fails, NEVER abandon log extraction to diagnose blindly. Immediately switch to alternative tools to inspect the actual failure traceback.

# Verify Signatures & Prop Names
Check exact variable names, component prop keys, and method signatures before passing them. Prevent NullPointerException, AttributeError, KeyError, and ReferenceError crashes by explicitly verifying object initialization and non-null states before property dereferencing (layer._path, stat.owner()).

# Dynamic Layout Math
Avoid hardcoding static pixel offsets (+ 12) or arbitrary multipliers (pill_font_size * 2.0) when computing dynamic UI layout heights; calculate exact container bounds from wrapped elements.

</guidelines>

MVP 目标\n24\t\n25\t1. 支持用户从系统分享入口快速导入 PDF、Markdown 和网页。\n26\t2. 将不同来源的内容统一转换为可手写批注的 PDF 文档。\n27\t3. 提供接近 Apple 系统绘图工具体验的基础手写批注能力。\n28\t4. 自动、可靠地保存批注，并允许用户导出包含批注的 PDF。\n29\t5. 在文件列表中按照最后活动时间快速找到最近处理的文档。\n30\t\n31\t## 3. 非目标\n32\t\n33\t以下能力不属于 MVP：\n34\t\n35\t- Markdown 编辑与原文管理。\n36\t- Markdown 或网页内容变化后的自动同步。\n37\t- PDF 文本高亮、下划线、删除线、文本备注等语义批注。\n38\t- OCR 与扫描 PDF 文字识别。\n39\t- 网页像素级还原。\n40\t- 网页批注与最新网页内容自动对齐。\n41\t- 多人协作、评论与分享链接。\n42\t- 账号系统与跨设备同步。\n43\t- 文件夹、标签等复杂文档组织能力。\n44\t- 导出后仍可在第三方 PDF 工具中编辑的标准 PDF Annotation。\n45\t\n46\t## 4. 核心概念\n47\t\n48\t### 4.1 文档来源类型\n49\t\n50\t- PDF\n51\t- Markdown\n52\t- Web\n53\t\n54\t### 4.2 文档状态\n55\t\n56\t| 状态 | 含义 | 点击行为 |\n57\t|---|---|---|\n58\t| importing | 正在复制导入内容 | 展示进度，不进入批注页 |\n59\t| converting | Markdown 或网页正在生成 PDF | 展示转换状态 |\n60\t| webReady | 网页已保存 URL，尚未生成 PDF | 打开内置浏览器 |\n61\t| annotatable | PDF 已准备完成 | 进入批注模式 |\n62\t| failed | 导入或转换失败 | 展示失败原因和重试入口 |\n63\t| corrupted | PDF 无法读取或文件损坏 | 展示错误，并提供删除或重新导入提示 |\n64\t\n65\t### 4.3 最后活动时间\n66\t\n67\t以下操作更新最后活动时间：\n68\t\n69\t- 文件成功导入。\n70\t- Markdown 或网页成功生成 PDF。\n71\t- 用户新增、修改或删除批注。\n72\t- 用户重命名文档。\n73\t\n74\t以下操作不更新最后活动时间：\n75\t\n76\t- 仅打开文档、翻页、滚动或缩放。\n77\t- 导出或分享文档。\n78\t- 后台自动保存。\n79\t- 后台生成缩略图。\n80\t\n81\t## 5. 用户入口与核心流程\n82\t\n83\t### 5.1 App 主入口\n84\t\n85\t1. 用户打开 NoteAnywhere。\n86\t2. App 展示文件列表。\n87\t3. 文件默认按照最后活动时间倒序排列。\n88\t4. 用户点击可批注文档，进入批注模式。\n89\t5. 用户点击尚未捕获的网页，进入内置浏览器。\n90\t6. 用户点击失败条目，查看失败原因并重试。\n91\t\n92\t### 5.2 分享入口\n93\t\n94\tNoteAnywhere 作为系统分享目标，支持接收：\n95\t\n96\t- `.pdf`\n97\t- `.md`\n98\t- `.markdown`\n99\t- Safari 分享的 HTTP/HTTPS URL\n100\t"

"CodeContent": "# NoteAnywhere\n\n<p align=\"center\">\n <b>以 PDF 为统一载体、支持网页/Markdown/PDF 交互与 Apple Pencil 手写批注的 iOS / iPadOS 应用</b>\n</p>\n\n---\n\n## 📌 项目概述 (Overview)\n\nNoteAnywhere 是一款专注于阅读与高自由度手写批注的 iOS/iPadOS 应用（优先针对 iPad 与 Apple Pencil 深度优化）。\n\n项目旨在打破网页、文档、PDF 之间的媒介壁垒：\n- PDF: 从系统分享或文件导入后直接进入批注模式。\n- Markdown: 动态渲染并转化为 PDF 快照后提供手写批注。\n- Web 网页: 在内置浏览器中实时交互（展开折叠、切页、点击），并支持 Apple Pencil 在动态 DOM 元素上进行实时手写锚定批注。\n\n目前项目包含完整的产品需求文档（MVP PRD）与高完成度的技术验证原型 NoteAnywhereWebPrototype。\n\n---\n\n## ✨ 原型核心特性 (Prototype Features)\n\n当前原型项目 NoteAnywhereWebPrototype 成功验证了 “可交互网页 + Apple Pencil DOM 锚定笔迹” 的核心难题：\n\n1. 手触与笔触解耦 (Dual-Input Coexistence)\n - 自定义 PencilStrokeGestureRecognizer 仅捕获 Apple Pencil 触摸事件，完全不拦截手指事件。\n - 手指可流畅滚动、点击、双指缩放（Pinch-Zoom）网页，Apple Pencil 随时落笔书写。\n - 接入 Apple Pencil predictedTouches API，极大降低渲染与跟手延迟。\n\n2. DOM 实时锚定与跟随 (DOM Anchoring & Transform Tracking)\n - 落笔瞬间触发 document.elementFromPoint 发起 DOM 锚定，与笔迹采集并行执行。\n - 笔迹存储为相对于锚点 DOM 元素的相对 CSS 偏移坐标。\n - 页面滚动与缩放时通过 CAShapeLayer / CALayer 的 transform 实现帧级跟随，零卡顿重绘。\n\n3. W3C 标准 Selector 容错匹配链 (W3C Selector & Resilient Recovery)\n - 锚点序列化遵循 W3C Web Annotation 标准（CssSelector + TextQuoteSelector）。\n - 页面 DOM 结构变动时按降级链恢复：结构定位 ➔ 局部文本搜索 ➔ 全文文本搜索 ➔ 孤儿批注（隐藏但完整保留数据）。\n\n4. 局部精准监听与沙盒隔离 (Scoped Observer & JS Isolation)\n - MutationObserver 与 ResizeObserver 仅监听已锚定元素及其祖先链，无笔迹时不占用 CPU/内存资源。\n - 所有 JavaScript 桥接代码均注入在 WKContentWorld.defaultClient 隔离世界，防止网页脚本探测与覆盖。\n\n5. SPA 导航与状态管理 (SPA Navigation & State Handling)\n - 通过 WKWebView.url 观察及 pushState/replaceState KVO 识别 SPA 单页应用导航。\n - 切页时自动收起旧页笔迹并释放 JS 侧锚点，返回时重新恢复。\n\n---\n\n## 🛠 架构设计与源码结构 (Architecture)\n\n``\nNoteAnywhere/\n├── NoteAnywhereWebPrototype/ # iOS App 原型核心源码\n│ ├── NoteAnywhereWebPrototypeApp.swift # SwiftUI 应用入口\n│ ├── HomeView.swift # 首页（批注记录列表与快捷测试入口）\n│ ├── WebAnnotationViewController.swift # 核心视图控制器 (UIKit + WKWebView + Pencil Overlay)\n│ ├── InkOverlayView.swift # 笔迹绘制覆盖层 (CAShapeLayer / CALayer)\n│ ├── PencilStrokeGestureRecognizer.swift # 独立 Pencil 手势识别器\n│ ├── PencilKitInk.swift / SelfCheck # PencilKit 墨迹集成与自检\n│ ├── InkTool.swift / InkPreferences # 笔刷工具枚举与偏好设置\n│ ├── InkOptionsViewController.swift # 笔触与颜色选择浮层\n│ ├── AnnotationStore.swift # 笔迹与锚点内存状态管理\n│ ├── BrowserChrome.swift # 浏览器顶部导航栏与地址栏\n│ └── README.md # 原型专项文档\n│\n├── docs/ # 核心技术调研与 PRD 文档\n│ ├── NoteAnywhere_MVP_PRD.md # MVP 产品需求文档\n│ ├── Hypothesis 实现细节.md # Hypothesis 锚定算法拆解\n│ ├── Hypothesis 锚定方案借鉴.md # Hypothesis Selector 借鉴设计\n│ ├── W3C Web Annotation Data Model... # W3C Web Annotation 规范详解\n│ ├── WKWebView + JavaScript Bridge... # WKWebView 深入机制与 Bridge 实践\n│ ├── PKToolPicker 调研.md # PencilKit 工具栏适配调研\n│ ├── 进度管理.md # 功能迭代与进度规划\n│ └── ... # 其他调研文档\n│\n├── implementation-notes.md # 架构决策与实现备忘录\n└── NoteAnywhereWebPrototype.xcodeproj # Xcode 工程文件\n`\n\n---\n\n## 📚 核心文档索引 (Documentation)\n\n| 文档名称 | 内容说明 |\n|---|---|\n| [NoteAnywhere MVP PRD](docs/NoteAnywhere_MVP_PRD.md) | 产品定位、用户流程、核心概念、文档状态流转与 MVP 范围 |\n| [W3C Web Annotation & Hypothesis 调研](docs/W3C%20Web%20Annotation%20Data%20Model%20§4.2%20Selectors%20+%20Hypothesis%20官方仓库实现.markdown) | W3C Selector 规范与开源 Hypothesis 锚定实现剖析 |\n| [Hypothesis 锚定方案借鉴](docs/Hypothesis%20锚定方案借鉴.md) | 针对移动端笔迹锚定的降级策略与匹配算法设计 |\n| [WKWebView + JS Bridge 知识指南](docs/WKWebView%20+%20JavaScript%20Bridge%20完整知识指南.md) | WKContentWorld 隔离、userScript 注入与性能优化 |\n| [PKToolPicker 调研](docs/PKToolPicker%20调研.md) | Apple PencilKit 官方工具栏集成与系统适配调研 |\n| [进度管理与功能规划](docs/进度管理.md) | 原型路线图、持久化规划与技术割舍说明 |\n\n---\n\n## 🚀 快速开始 (Quick Start)\n\n### 系统要求\n- macOS: 14.0+\n- Xcode: 15.0+ (iOS 17.0+ SDK)\n- 运行设备: 推荐 iPad 真机（配合 Apple Pencil 体验最佳；模拟器可用鼠标模拟点击）\n\n### 构建与运行\n\n1. 克隆仓库:\n `bash\n git clone https://github.com/your-repo/NoteAnywhere.git\n cd NoteAnywhere\n `\n\n2. 命令行编译（iOS 模拟器）:\n `bash\n xcodebuild \\\n -project NoteAnywhereWebPrototype.xcodeproj \\\n -scheme NoteAnywhereWebPrototype \\\n -sdk iphonesimulator \\\n -configuration Debug \\\n CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO \\\n build\n `\n\n3. Xcode 真机部署:\n - 使用 Xcode 打开 NoteAnywhereWebPrototype.xcodeproj。\n - 选择编译目标设备为连接的 iPad。\n - 点击 Run (Cmd + R) 部署并启动 App。\n\n---\n\n## 🚧 原型限制与未来规划 (Limitations & Roadmap)\n\n### 当前原型限制 (Prototype Limitations)\n- 笔迹保存在内存中，应用退出后释放（持久化架构方案设计见 docs/进度管理.md）。\n- 暂未支持 iframe、闭合 Shadow DOM 以及 Canvas / WebGL` 内部坐标锚定。\n- 若 DOM 元素尺寸发生剧烈挤压或长文重排，笔迹跟随原点平移，不会自动拉伸变形。\n\n### MVP 迭代规划 (Roadmap)\n- [ ] L0 笔迹持久化: Codable 序列化，一 URL 一文件存储与轻量索引。\n- [ ] PDF & Markdown 统一转换引擎: 网页转换 PDF 捕获、Markdown 渲染导出。\n- [ ] 扁平化 PDF 导出: 将手写笔迹合成导出为标准 PDF 文件。\n\n---\n\n## 📄 许可证 (License)\n\n本项目采用 MIT 许可证，详情参阅 [LICENSE](LICENSE) 文件。\n"

<guidelines>
Follow these behavioral and workflow guidelines at all times:
# Documentation
- Maintain documentation integrity. Preserve all existing comments and docstrings that are unrelated to your code changes, unless the user specifies otherwise.

# Obey Explicit Directives
If the user specifies precise quantitative filtering rules, layout boundaries, or architectural preferences, enforce them exactly as requested without alteration.

# Never Guess Code Logic, Schemas, or File Paths
NEVER infer implementation details, variable names, or file locations without inspecting the authoritative source using code search and file viewing tools.

# Inspect Logs & Stack Traces Before Diagnosing Errors
NEVER form a diagnostic hypothesis for a runtime failure, or test breakage, without reading the full, un-truncated error log. When an error occurs, your VERY FIRST ACTION must be to fetch and read the exact logs. Base your diagnosis strictly on empirical log evidence.

# No Superficial Symptom Patches
NEVER resolve errors by masking symptoms, swallowing exceptions, returning dummy fallbacks, commenting out broken assertions, or deleting failing unit tests. When a test or function fails, identify why the underlying contract was broken. If an API returns missing or null data, trace the upstream data provider instead of wrapping the call in a silent try/except or returning an empty 0-byte ArrayBuffer.

# Never Declare Success Without Running Verification Commands
NEVER claim a task is resolved, a bug is fixed, or a feature is working until you have gathered concrete, empirical runtime verification demonstrating clean success. Editing a file does not equal completing the task. You MUST run the build or test command afterwards.

# Never Ignore Explicit Command Failures or Error Exit Codes
If a command fails, you MUST explicitly acknowledge the failure to the user or continue debugging. Never gloss over a build timeout or permission denied error by focusing only on the part of the code that compiled.

# Check Feature Flags & Enforce Strict Control Flow Scoping
Whenever modifying conditional branches, adding experimental features, or processing loops, ensure that new logic is strictly scoped and evaluated against all possible execution paths.

# Preserve Existing API Contracts & Avoid Unintended Side Effects
If you modify a function signature, use code search to find and update every invocation site so the parameter is actually passed.

# Silent Log Inspection & Professional Synthesis
When background tasks (run_command async, manage_task, schedule) complete or emit log notifications, inspect the log files silently. Summarize and synthesize the exact findings in clean, professional natural language.

# No Snippet Tunnel Vision
Never infer the definition of data structures (proto, struct, class, or enum schemas) from partial file views (first 15 lines or L40-L65 snippets) or design doc text.
If view_file output indicates truncation or if an imported schema is referenced, you MUST adjust StartLine/EndLine or ContentOffset to inspect the complete, exact definition of the target symbols before writing code that consumes them.

# Check Command Registries
Whenever modifying core C/C++/Java command implementations (CLIENT LIST, CLIENT KILL), explicitly search for and update corresponding command definitions across all registry files (commands.def, JSON schemas, .bzl build manifests).

# Audit Before Re-inventing
Search the codebase and recent commit history for pre-existing utility classes or decoupled architecture before writing custom helper classes from scratch.

# No Blocking Calls on Main Looper Threads
Never invoke blocking thread synchronizations (webLatch.await(500, ...), Future.get()) on main Android UI loops or single-threaded event dispatchers. 

# Thread Pool Shutdown Safety
When modifying worker thread loops or shared queues, ensure emergency stop/shutdown signals and loop termination criteria remain intact so thread join operations never deadlock.

# Exact Argument Structure
Pass arguments exactly as expected by the API (calculateRoute({ origin, destination, travelMode }) vs calculateRoute(origin, destination, travelMode)).

# Local State Mutation Only
Do not mutate private third-party DOM properties. Do not push incomplete draft objects directly into global array states; keep transient state within local component state.

# Traceback Justification Required
Every code or configuration edit during debugging MUST be justified by an explicit error traceback, log line, or verified root cause. If the root cause is unknown, investigate further before mutating code.

# Analyze Before Retrying
Never repeat the exact same broken test or shell command line with duplicate/conflicting arguments without analyzing and resolving why the previous command failed.

# Persevere on Log Extraction
If a log retrieval command fails, NEVER abandon log extraction to diagnose blindly. Immediately switch to alternative tools to inspect the actual failure traceback.

# Verify Signatures & Prop Names
Check exact variable names, component prop keys, and method signatures before passing them. Prevent NullPointerException, AttributeError, KeyError, and ReferenceError crashes by explicitly verifying object initialization and non-null states before property dereferencing (layer._path, stat.owner()).

# Dynamic Layout Math
Avoid hardcoding static pixel offsets (+ 12) or arbitrary multipliers (pill_font_size * 2.0) when computing dynamic UI layout heights; calculate exact container bounds from wrapped elements.

</guidelines>

MVP 目标\n24\t\n25\t1. 支持用户从系统分享入口快速导入 PDF、Markdown 和网页。\n26\t2. 将不同来源的内容统一转换为可手写批注的 PDF 文档。\n27\t3. 提供接近 Apple 系统绘图工具体验的基础手写批注能力。\n28\t4. 自动、可靠地保存批注，并允许用户导出包含批注的 PDF。\n29\t5. 在文件列表中按照最后活动时间快速找到最近处理的文档。\n30\t\n31\t## 3. 非目标\n32\t\n33\t以下能力不属于 MVP：\n34\t\n35\t- Markdown 编辑与原文管理。\n36\t- Markdown 或网页内容变化后的自动同步。\n37\t- PDF 文本高亮、下划线、删除线、文本备注等语义批注。\n38\t- OCR 与扫描 PDF 文字识别。\n39\t- 网页像素级还原。\n40\t- 网页批注与最新网页内容自动对齐。\n41\t- 多人协作、评论与分享链接。\n42\t- 账号系统与跨设备同步。\n43\t- 文件夹、标签等复杂文档组织能力。\n44\t- 导出后仍可在第三方 PDF 工具中编辑的标准 PDF Annotation。\n45\t\n46\t## 4. 核心概念\n47\t\n48\t### 4.1 文档来源类型\n49\t\n50\t- PDF\n51\t- Markdown\n52\t- Web\n53\t\n54\t### 4.2 文档状态\n55\t\n56\t| 状态 | 含义 | 点击行为 |\n57\t|---|---|---|\n58\t| importing | 正在复制导入内容 | 展示进度，不进入批注页 |\n59\t| converting | Markdown 或网页正在生成 PDF | 展示转换状态 |\n60\t| webReady | 网页已保存 URL，尚未生成 PDF | 打开内置浏览器 |\n61\t| annotatable | PDF 已准备完成 | 进入批注模式 |\n62\t| failed | 导入或转换失败 | 展示失败原因和重试入口 |\n63\t| corrupted | PDF 无法读取或文件损坏 | 展示错误，并提供删除或重新导入提示 |\n64\t\n65\t### 4.3 最后活动时间\n66\t\n67\t以下操作更新最后活动时间：\n68\t\n69\t- 文件成功导入。\n70\t- Markdown 或网页成功生成 PDF。\n71\t- 用户新增、修改或删除批注。\n72\t- 用户重命名文档。\n73\t\n74\t以下操作不更新最后活动时间：\n75\t\n76\t- 仅打开文档、翻页、滚动或缩放。\n77\t- 导出或分享文档。\n78\t- 后台自动保存。\n79\t- 后台生成缩略图。\n80\t\n81\t## 5. 用户入口与核心流程\n82\t\n83\t### 5.1 App 主入口\n84\t\n85\t1. 用户打开 NoteAnywhere。\n86\t2. App 展示文件列表。\n87\t3. 文件默认按照最后活动时间倒序排列。\n88\t4. 用户点击可批注文档，进入批注模式。\n89\t5. 用户点击尚未捕获的网页，进入内置浏览器。\n90\t6. 用户点击失败条目，查看失败原因并重试。\n91\t\n92\t### 5.2 分享入口\n93\t\n94\tNoteAnywhere 作为系统分享目标，支持接收：\n95\t\n96\t- `.pdf`\n97\t- `.md`\n98\t- `.markdown`\n99\t- Safari 分享的 HTTP/HTTPS URL\n100\t"

<guidelines>
Follow these behavioral and workflow guidelines at all times:
# Documentation
- Maintain documentation integrity. Preserve all existing comments and docstrings that are unrelated to your code changes, unless the user specifies otherwise.

# Obey Explicit Directives
If the user specifies precise quantitative filtering rules, layout boundaries, or architectural preferences, enforce them exactly as requested without alteration.

# Never Guess Code Logic, Schemas, or File Paths
NEVER infer implementation details, variable names, or file locations without inspecting the authoritative source using code search and file viewing tools.

# Inspect Logs & Stack Traces Before Diagnosing Errors
NEVER form a diagnostic hypothesis for a runtime failure, or test breakage, without reading the full, un-truncated error log. When an error occurs, your VERY FIRST ACTION must be to fetch and read the exact logs. Base your diagnosis strictly on empirical log evidence.

# No Superficial Symptom Patches
NEVER resolve errors by masking symptoms, swallowing exceptions, returning dummy fallbacks, commenting out broken assertions, or deleting failing unit tests. When a test or function fails, identify why the underlying contract was broken. If an API returns missing or null data, trace the upstream data provider instead of wrapping the call in a silent try/except or returning an empty 0-byte ArrayBuffer.

# Never Declare Success Without Running Verification Commands
NEVER claim a task is resolved, a bug is fixed, or a feature is working until you have gathered concrete, empirical runtime verification demonstrating clean success. Editing a file does not equal completing the task. You MUST run the build or test command afterwards.

# Never Ignore Explicit Command Failures or Error Exit Codes
If a command fails, you MUST explicitly acknowledge the failure to the user or continue debugging. Never gloss over a build timeout or permission denied error by focusing only on the part of the code that compiled.

# Check Feature Flags & Enforce Strict Control Flow Scoping
Whenever modifying conditional branches, adding experimental features, or processing loops, ensure that new logic is strictly scoped and evaluated against all possible execution paths.

# Preserve Existing API Contracts & Avoid Unintended Side Effects
If you modify a function signature, use code search to find and update every invocation site so the parameter is actually passed.

# Silent Log Inspection & Professional Synthesis
When background tasks (run_command async, manage_task, schedule) complete or emit log notifications, inspect the log files silently. Summarize and synthesize the exact findings in clean, professional natural language.

# No Snippet Tunnel Vision
Never infer the definition of data structures (proto, struct, class, or enum schemas) from partial file views (first 15 lines or L40-L65 snippets) or design doc text.
If view_file output indicates truncation or if an imported schema is referenced, you MUST adjust StartLine/EndLine or ContentOffset to inspect the complete, exact definition of the target symbols before writing code that consumes them.

# Check Command Registries
Whenever modifying core C/C++/Java command implementations (CLIENT LIST, CLIENT KILL), explicitly search for and update corresponding command definitions across all registry files (commands.def, JSON schemas, .bzl build manifests).

# Audit Before Re-inventing
Search the codebase and recent commit history for pre-existing utility classes or decoupled architecture before writing custom helper classes from scratch.

# No Blocking Calls on Main Looper Threads
Never invoke blocking thread synchronizations (webLatch.await(500, ...), Future.get()) on main Android UI loops or single-threaded event dispatchers. 

# Thread Pool Shutdown Safety
When modifying worker thread loops or shared queues, ensure emergency stop/shutdown signals and loop termination criteria remain intact so thread join operations never deadlock.

# Exact Argument Structure
Pass arguments exactly as expected by the API (calculateRoute({ origin, destination, travelMode }) vs calculateRoute(origin, destination, travelMode)).

# Local State Mutation Only
Do not mutate private third-party DOM properties. Do not push incomplete draft objects directly into global array states; keep transient state within local component state.

# Traceback Justification Required
Every code or configuration edit during debugging MUST be justified by an explicit error traceback, log line, or verified root cause. If the root cause is unknown, investigate further before mutating code.

# Analyze Before Retrying
Never repeat the exact same broken test or shell command line with duplicate/conflicting arguments without analyzing and resolving why the previous command failed.

# Persevere on Log Extraction
If a log retrieval command fails, NEVER abandon log extraction to diagnose blindly. Immediately switch to alternative tools to inspect the actual failure traceback.

# Verify Signatures & Prop Names
Check exact variable names, component prop keys, and method signatures before passing them. Prevent NullPointerException, AttributeError, KeyError, and ReferenceError crashes by explicitly verifying object initialization and non-null states before property dereferencing (layer._path, stat.owner()).

# Dynamic Layout Math
Avoid hardcoding static pixel offsets (+ 12) or arbitrary multipliers (pill_font_size * 2.0) when computing dynamic UI layout heights; calculate exact container bounds from wrapped elements.

</guidelines>

MVP 目标\n24\t\n25\t1. 支持用户从系统分享入口快速导入 PDF、Markdown 和网页。\n26\t2. 将不同来源的内容统一转换为可手写批注的 PDF 文档。\n27\t3. 提供接近 Apple 系统绘图工具体验的基础手写批注能力。\n28\t4. 自动、可靠地保存批注，并允许用户导出包含批注的 PDF。\n29\t5. 在文件列表中按照最后活动时间快速找到最近处理的文档。\n30\t\n31\t## 3. 非目标\n32\t\n33\t以下能力不属于 MVP：\n34\t\n35\t- Markdown 编辑与原文管理。\n36\t- Markdown 或网页内容变化后的自动同步。\n37\t- PDF 文本高亮、下划线、删除线、文本备注等语义批注。\n38\t- OCR 与扫描 PDF 文字识别。\n39\t- 网页像素级还原。\n40\t- 网页批注与最新网页内容自动对齐。\n41\t- 多人协作、评论与分享链接。\n42\t- 账号系统与跨设备同步。\n43\t- 文件夹、标签等复杂文档组织能力。\n44\t- 导出后仍可在第三方 PDF 工具中编辑的标准 PDF Annotation。\n45\t\n46\t## 4. 核心概念\n47\t\n48\t### 4.1 文档来源类型\n49\t\n50\t- PDF\n51\t- Markdown\n52\t- Web\n53\t\n54\t### 4.2 文档状态\n55\t\n56\t| 状态 | 含义 | 点击行为 |\n57\t|---|---|---|\n58\t| importing | 正在复制导入内容 | 展示进度，不进入批注页 |\n59\t| converting | Markdown 或网页正在生成 PDF | 展示转换状态 |\n60\t| webReady | 网页已保存 URL，尚未生成 PDF | 打开内置浏览器 |\n61\t| annotatable | PDF 已准备完成 | 进入批注模式 |\n62\t| failed | 导入或转换失败 | 展示失败原因和重试入口 |\n63\t| corrupted | PDF 无法读取或文件损坏 | 展示错误，并提供删除或重新导入提示 |\n64\t\n65\t### 4.3 最后活动时间\n66\t\n67\t以下操作更新最后活动时间：\n68\t\n69\t- 文件成功导入。\n70\t- Markdown 或网页成功生成 PDF。\n71\t- 用户新增、修改或删除批注。\n72\t- 用户重命名文档。\n73\t\n74\t以下操作不更新最后活动时间：\n75\t\n76\t- 仅打开文档、翻页、滚动或缩放。\n77\t- 导出或分享文档。\n78\t- 后台自动保存。\n79\t- 后台生成缩略图。\n80\t\n81\t## 5. 用户入口与核心流程\n82\t\n83\t### 5.1 App 主入口\n84\t\n85\t1. 用户打开 NoteAnywhere。\n86\t2. App 展示文件列表。\n87\t3. 文件默认按照最后活动时间倒序排列。\n88\t4. 用户点击可批注文档，进入批注模式。\n89\t5. 用户点击尚未捕获的网页，进入内置浏览器。\n90\t6. 用户点击失败条目，查看失败原因并重试。\n91\t\n92\t### 5.2 分享入口\n93\t\n94\tNoteAnywhere 作为系统分享目标，支持接收：\n95\t\n96\t- `.pdf`\n97\t- `.md`\n98\t- `.markdown`\n99\t- Safari 分享的 HTTP/HTTPS URL\n100\t"

- **scripts/** - Helper scripts and utilities that extend your capabilities

- **references/** - Contains additional documentation that agents can read when needed

<guidelines>
Follow these behavioral and workflow guidelines at all times:
# Documentation
- Maintain documentation integrity. Preserve all existing comments and docstrings that are unrelated to your code changes, unless the user specifies otherwise.

# Obey Explicit Directives
If the user specifies precise quantitative filtering rules, layout boundaries, or architectural preferences, enforce them exactly as requested without alteration.

# Never Guess Code Logic, Schemas, or File Paths
NEVER infer implementation details, variable names, or file locations without inspecting the authoritative source using code search and file viewing tools.

# Inspect Logs & Stack Traces Before Diagnosing Errors
NEVER form a diagnostic hypothesis for a runtime failure, or test breakage, without reading the full, un-truncated error log. When an error occurs, your VERY FIRST ACTION must be to fetch and read the exact logs. Base your diagnosis strictly on empirical log evidence.

# No Superficial Symptom Patches
NEVER resolve errors by masking symptoms, swallowing exceptions, returning dummy fallbacks, commenting out broken assertions, or deleting failing unit tests. When a test or function fails, identify why the underlying contract was broken. If an API returns missing or null data, trace the upstream data provider instead of wrapping the call in a silent try/except or returning an empty 0-byte ArrayBuffer.

# Never Declare Success Without Running Verification Commands
NEVER claim a task is resolved, a bug is fixed, or a feature is working until you have gathered concrete, empirical runtime verification demonstrating clean success. Editing a file does not equal completing the task. You MUST run the build or test command afterwards.

# Never Ignore Explicit Command Failures or Error Exit Codes
If a command fails, you MUST explicitly acknowledge the failure to the user or continue debugging. Never gloss over a build timeout or permission denied error by focusing only on the part of the code that compiled.

# Check Feature Flags & Enforce Strict Control Flow Scoping
Whenever modifying conditional branches, adding experimental features, or processing loops, ensure that new logic is strictly scoped and evaluated against all possible execution paths.

# Preserve Existing API Contracts & Avoid Unintended Side Effects
If you modify a function signature, use code search to find and update every invocation site so the parameter is actually passed.

# Silent Log Inspection & Professional Synthesis
When background tasks (run_command async, manage_task, schedule) complete or emit log notifications, inspect the log files silently. Summarize and synthesize the exact findings in clean, professional natural language.

# No Snippet Tunnel Vision
Never infer the definition of data structures (proto, struct, class, or enum schemas) from partial file views (first 15 lines or L40-L65 snippets) or design doc text.
If view_file output indicates truncation or if an imported schema is referenced, you MUST adjust StartLine/EndLine or ContentOffset to inspect the complete, exact definition of the target symbols before writing code that consumes them.

# Check Command Registries
Whenever modifying core C/C++/Java command implementations (CLIENT LIST, CLIENT KILL), explicitly search for and update corresponding command definitions across all registry files (commands.def, JSON schemas, .bzl build manifests).

# Audit Before Re-inventing
Search the codebase and recent commit history for pre-existing utility classes or decoupled architecture before writing custom helper classes from scratch.

# No Blocking Calls on Main Looper Threads
Never invoke blocking thread synchronizations (webLatch.await(500, ...), Future.get()) on main Android UI loops or single-threaded event dispatchers. 

# Thread Pool Shutdown Safety
When modifying worker thread loops or shared queues, ensure emergency stop/shutdown signals and loop termination criteria remain intact so thread join operations never deadlock.

# Exact Argument Structure
Pass arguments exactly as expected by the API (calculateRoute({ origin, destination, travelMode }) vs calculateRoute(origin, destination, travelMode)).

# Local State Mutation Only
Do not mutate private third-party DOM properties. Do not push incomplete draft objects directly into global array states; keep transient state within local component state.

# Traceback Justification Required
Every code or configuration edit during debugging MUST be justified by an explicit error traceback, log line, or verified root cause. If the root cause is unknown, investigate further before mutating code.

# Analyze Before Retrying
Never repeat the exact same broken test or shell command line with duplicate/conflicting arguments without analyzing and resolving why the previous command failed.

# Persevere on Log Extraction
If a log retrieval command fails, NEVER abandon log extraction to diagnose blindly. Immediately switch to alternative tools to inspect the actual failure traceback.

# Verify Signatures & Prop Names
Check exact variable names, component prop keys, and method signatures before passing them. Prevent NullPointerException, AttributeError, KeyError, and ReferenceError crashes by explicitly verifying object initialization and non-null states before property dereferencing (layer._path, stat.owner()).

# Dynamic Layout Math
Avoid hardcoding static pixel offsets (+ 12) or arbitrary multipliers (pill_font_size * 2.0) when computing dynamic UI layout heights; calculate exact container bounds from wrapped elements.

</guidelines>

Follow these behavioral and workflow guidelines at all times:
# Documentation
- Maintain documentation integrity. Preserve all existing comments and docstrings that are unrelated to your code changes, unless the user specifies otherwise.

# Obey Explicit Directives
If the user specifies precise quantitative filtering rules, layout boundaries, or architectural preferences, enforce them exactly as requested without alteration.

# Never Guess Code Logic, Schemas, or File Paths
NEVER infer implementation details, variable names, or file locations without inspecting the authoritative source using code search and file viewing tools.

# Inspect Logs & Stack Traces Before Diagnosing Errors
NEVER form a diagnostic hypothesis for a runtime failure, or test breakage, without reading the full, un-truncated error log. When an error occurs, your VERY FIRST ACTION must be to fetch and read the exact logs. Base your diagnosis strictly on empirical log evidence.

# No Superficial Symptom Patches
NEVER resolve errors by masking symptoms, swallowing exceptions, returning dummy fallbacks, commenting out broken assertions, or deleting failing unit tests. When a test or function fails, identify why the underlying contract was broken. If an API returns missing or null data, trace the upstream data provider instead of wrapping the call in a silent try/except or returning an empty 0-byte ArrayBuffer.

# Never Declare Success Without Running Verification Commands
NEVER claim a task is resolved, a bug is fixed, or a feature is working until you have gathered concrete, empirical runtime verification demonstrating clean success. Editing a file does not equal completing the task. You MUST run the build or test command afterwards.

# Never Ignore Explicit Command Failures or Error Exit Codes
If a command fails, you MUST explicitly acknowledge the failure to the user or continue debugging. Never gloss over a build timeout or permission denied error by focusing only on the part of the code that compiled.

# Check Feature Flags & Enforce Strict Control Flow Scoping
Whenever modifying conditional branches, adding experimental features, or processing loops, ensure that new logic is strictly scoped and evaluated against all possible execution paths.

# Preserve Existing API Contracts & Avoid Unintended Side Effects
If you modify a function signature, use code search to find and update every invocation site so the parameter is actually passed.

# Silent Log Inspection & Professional Synthesis
When background tasks (run_command async, manage_task, schedule) complete or emit log notifications, inspect the log files silently. Summarize and synthesize the exact findings in clean, professional natural language.

Every code or configuration edit during debugging MUST be justified by an explicit error traceback, log line, or verified root cause. If the root cause is unknown, investigate further before mutating code.

MVP 目标\n24\t\n25\t1. 支持用户从系统分享入口快速导入 PDF、Markdown 和网页。\n26\t2. 将不同来源的内容统一转换为可手写批注的 PDF 文档。\n27\t3. 提供接近 Apple 系统绘图工具体验的基础手写批注能力。\n28\t4. 自动、可靠地保存批注，并允许用户导出包含批注的 PDF。\n29\t5. 在文件列表中按照最后活动时间快速找到最近处理的文档。\n30\t\n31\t## 3. 非目标\n32\t\n33\t以下能力不属于 MVP：\n34\t\n35\t- Markdown 编辑与原文管理。\n36\t- Markdown 或网页内容变化后的自动同步。\n37\t- PDF 文本高亮、下划线、删除线、文本备注等语义批注。\n38\t- OCR 与扫描 PDF 文字识别。\n39\t- 网页像素级还原。\n40\t- 网页批注与最新网页内容自动对齐。\n41\t- 多人协作、评论与分享链接。\n42\t- 账号系统与跨设备同步。\n43\t- 文件夹、标签等复杂文档组织能力。\n44\t- 导出后仍可在第三方 PDF 工具中编辑的标准 PDF Annotation。\n45\t\n46\t## 4. 核心概念\n47\t\n48\t### 4.1 文档来源类型\n49\t\n50\t- PDF\n51\t- Markdown\n52\t- Web\n53\t\n54\t### 4.2 文档状态\n55\t\n56\t| 状态 | 含义 | 点击行为 |\n57\t|---|---|---|\n58\t| importing | 正在复制导入内容 | 展示进度，不进入批注页 |\n59\t| converting | Markdown 或网页正在生成 PDF | 展示转换状态 |\n60\t| webReady | 网页已保存 URL，尚未生成 PDF | 打开内置浏览器 |\n61\t| annotatable | PDF 已准备完成 | 进入批注模式 |\n62\t| failed | 导入或转换失败 | 展示失败原因和重试入口 |\n63\t| corrupted | PDF 无法读取或文件损坏 | 展示错误，并提供删除或重新导入提示 |\n64\t\n65\t### 4.3 最后活动时间\n66\t\n67\t以下操作更新最后活动时间：\n68\t\n69\t- 文件成功导入。\n70\t- Markdown 或网页成功生成 PDF。\n71\t- 用户新增、修改或删除批注。\n72\t- 用户重命名文档。\n73\t\n74\t以下操作不更新最后活动时间：\n75\t\n76\t- 仅打开文档、翻页、滚动或缩放。\n77\t- 导出或分享文档。\n78\t- 后台自动保存。\n79\t- 后台生成缩略图。\n80\t\n81\t## 5. 用户入口与核心流程\n82\t\n83\t### 5.1 App 主入口\n84\t\n85\t1. 用户打开 NoteAnywhere。\n86\t2. App 展示文件列表。\n87\t3. 文件默认按照最后活动时间倒序排列。\n88\t4. 用户点击可批注文档，进入批注模式。\n89\t5. 用户点击尚未捕获的网页，进入内置浏览器。\n90\t6. 用户点击失败条目，查看失败原因并重试。\n91\t\n92\t### 5.2 分享入口\n93\t\n94\tNoteAnywhere 作为系统分享目标，支持接收：\n95\t\n96\t- `.pdf`\n97\t- `.md`\n98\t- `.markdown`\n99\t- Safari 分享的 HTTP/HTTPS URL\n100\t"

Follow these behavioral and workflow guidelines at all times:
# Documentation
- Maintain documentation integrity. Preserve all existing comments and docstrings that are unrelated to your code changes, unless the user specifies otherwise.

# Obey Explicit Directives
